DC power flow constraints\n    \"\"\"\n    n_gen = net['n_gen']\n    n_bus = net['n_bus']\n    B = net['B']\n    slack_bus = net['slack_bus']\n\n    # For linear programming, we'll use linear costs\n    # Convert quadratic costs to linear by linearizing around an operating point\n    # Using marginal cost at Pmin as approximation\n    c_linear = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c2, c1, c0 = net['gen_costs'][i, 2], net['gen_costs'][i, 1], net['gen_costs'][i, 0]\n        # Marginal cost = 2*c2*P + c1, use Pmin as starting point\n        p_min = net['gen_pmin'][i]\n        marginal_cost = 2 * c2 * p_min + c1\n        c_linear.append(marginal_cost)\n\n    # Variables: [P_g[0], ..., P_g[n-1], R_g[0], ..., R_g[n-1]]\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen\n\n    # Objective function coefficients\n    c_obj = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c_obj[i] = c_linear[i]  # Cost for P_g\n        c_obj[i + n_gen] = c_linear[i] * 0.1  # Cost for reserves (10% of energy cost)\n\n    # Constraint matrices\n    # 1. Power balance constraint\n    # Remove slack bus row from B matrix for power balance\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B, slack_bus, axis=0)\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B_reduced, slack_bus, axis=1)\n\n    # Get generator incidence matrix (which generators connect to which buses)\n    # For power balance at each bus: sum of P_g at bus - load - sum of flows = 0\n    A_eq = np.zeros((n_bus - 1, n_vars))\n    b_eq = np.zeros(n_bus - 1)\n\n    # Build power balance constraints\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        if bus_idx == slack_bus:\n            continue\n\n        row_idx = bus_idx if bus_idx < slack_bus else bus_idx - 1\n\n        # Generators at this bus\n        for gen_idx, gen_bus in enumerate(net['gen_buses']):\n            if gen_bus - 1 == bus_idx:\n                A_eq[row_idx, gen_idx] = 1.0  # P_g contributes to power balance\n\n        # Load at this bus\n        b_eq[row_idx] = net['loads'][bus_idx]\n\n    # 2. Generator Pmin/Pmax constraints\n    # Pmin <= P_g <= Pmax\n    A_ub_pmin = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmin = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmin[i, i] = 1.0  # P_g >= Pmin\n        b_ub_pmin[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    A_ub_pmax = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmax = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmax[i, i] = -1.0  # -P_g >= -Pmax\n        b_ub_pmax[i] = net['gen_pmin'][i]\n\n    # 3. Reserve constraints\n    # R_g >= 0\n    A_ub_r_min = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_min[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0\n        b_ub_r_min[i] = 0\n\n    # R_g <= Pmax - P_g\n    A_ub_r_max = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i] = -1.0  # -P_g\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0  # +R_g\n        b_ub_r_max[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 4. Spinning reserve requirement\n    # sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    min_reserves = RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n\n    A_ub_res = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_ub_res = np.array([min_reserves])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_res[0, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # Variable bounds\n    bounds = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((net['gen_pmin'][i], net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # P_g bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((0, net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # Use linprog with sparse matrices for efficiency\n    result = linprog(\n        c=c_obj,\n        A_ub=A_ub if A_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        b_ub=b_ub if b_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        A_eq=A_eq if A_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        b_eq=b_eq if b_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        bounds=bounds,\n        method='highs',\n        options={'disp': True}\n    )\n\n    if result.success:\n        print(\"Optimization successful!\")\n        p_gen = result.x[:net['n_gen']]\n        r_gen = result.x[net['n_gen']:]\n\n        return p_gen, r_gen, result\n    else:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n        return None, None, result\n\ndef calculate_line_flows(net, p_gen):\n    \"\"\"Calculate DC power flow and line loading\"\"\"\n    n_bus = net['n_bus']\n    n_branch = net['n_branch']\n    slack_bus = net['slack_bus']\n\n    # Build generator injection vector\n    P_gen_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(net['gen_buses']):\n        P_gen_bus[int(bus) - 1] += p_gen[i]\n\n    # Build load vector\n    P_load_bus = net['loads']\n\n    # Net injection at each bus\n    P_net = P_gen_bus - P_load_bus\n\n    # Solve DC power flow: B * theta = P_net (excluding slack)\n    B = net['B']\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B, slack_bus, axis=0)\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B_reduced, slack_bus, axis=1)\n\n    P_net_reduced = np.delete(P_net, slack_bus)\n\n    # Solve for voltage angles\n    theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_net_reduced)\n    theta = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta[np.arange(n_bus) != slack_bus] = theta_reduced\n    theta[slack_bus] = 0\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    flows = []\n    from_bus = net['from_bus']\n    to_bus = net['to_bus']\n    rate_a = net['rate_a']\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus:\n            flow = (theta[int(from_bus[i])] - theta[int(to_bus[i])]) / x[i]\n            flow_mw = flow * net['base_mva']\n            loading_pct = abs(flow_mw / rate_a[i]) * 100 if rate_a[i] > 0 else 0\n\n            flows.append({\n                'from': int(from_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'to': int(to_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'flow_MW': flow_mw,\n                'rating_MVA': rate_a[i],\n                'loading_pct': loading_pct\n            })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting Grid Dispatch Economic Dispatch Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # P_g[i] for each generator i (real power output)\n    - R_g[i] for each generator i (spinning reserves)\n\n    Objective: Minimize generation cost\n    Minimize sum(c2_i * P_g[i]^2 + c1_i * P_g[i] + c0_i + reserve_cost_factor * R_g[i])\n\n    Constraints:\n    1. Power balance: sum(P_g) - sum(loads) - losses = 0\n    2. Generator limits: Pmin <= P_g <= Pmax\n    3. Reserve limits: 0 <= R_g <= min(Pmax - P_g, reserve_cap)\n    4. Build generator injection vector\n    P_gen_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(net['gen_buses']):\n        P_gen_bus[int(bus) - 1] += p_gen[i]\n\n    # Build load vector\n    P_load_bus = net['loads']\n\n    # Net injection at each bus\n    P_net = P_gen_bus - P_load_bus\n\n    # Solve DC power flow: B * theta = P_net (excluding slack)\n    B = net['B']\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B, slack_bus, axis=0)\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B_reduced, slack_bus, axis=1)\n\n    P_net_reduced = np.delete(P_net, slack_bus)\n\n    # Solve for voltage angles\n    theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_net_reduced)\n    theta = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta[np.arange(n_bus) != slack_bus] = theta_reduced\n    theta[slack_bus] = 0\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    flows = []\n    from_bus = net['from_bus']\n    to_bus = net['to_bus']\n    rate_a = net['rate_a']\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus:\n            flow = (theta[int(from_bus[i])] - theta[int(to_bus[i])]) / x[i]\n            flow_mw = flow * net['base_mva']\n            loading_pct = abs(flow_mw / rate_a[i]) * 100 if rate_a[i] > 0 else 0\n\n            flows.append({\n                'from': int(from_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'to': int(to_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'flow_MW': flow_mw,\n                'rating_MVA': rate_a[i],\n                'loading_pct': loading_pct\n            })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting Grid Dispatch Economic Dispatch Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"\\n1. Loading network data...\")\n    net = parse_network('/root/network.json')\n    print(f\"   - Buses: {net['n_bus']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Generators: {net['n_gen']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Transmission lines: {net['n_branch']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Total load: {np.sum(net['loads']):.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Solve dispatch\n    print(\"\\n2. Solving economic dispatch...\")\n    p_gen, r_gen, result = solve_dispatch(net)\n\n    if p_gen is None:\n        print(\"ERROR: Dispatch solution failed\")\n        return\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    print(\"\\n3. Calculating transmission line flows...\")\n    flows = calculate_line_flows(net, p_gen)\n\n    # Prepare results\n    generator_dispatch = []\n    total_cost = 0\n    total_gen = 0\n    total_reserve = 0\n\n    for i in range(net['n_gen']):\n        bus_id = int(net['gen_buses'][i])\n        pmax = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n        p_min = net['gen_pmin'][i]\n        output_mw = p_gen[i]\n        reserve_mw = r_gen[i]\n\n        # Calculate cost (using actual quadratic cost)\n        c0, c1, c2 = net['gen_costs'][i]\n        cost = c2 * output_mw**2 + c1 * output_mw + c0\n\n        total_cost += cost\n        total_gen += output_mw\n        total_reserve += reserve_mw\n\n        generator_dispatch.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'output_MW': float(output_mw),\n            'reserve_MW': float(reserve_mw),\n            'pmax_MW': float(pmax)\n        })\n\n    # Sort lines by loading\n    flows_sorted = sorted(flows, key=lambda x: abs(x['loading_pct']), reverse=True)\n    most_loaded_lines = flows_sorted[:3]\n\n    # Calculate operating margin\n    total_capacity = np.sum(net['gen_pmax'])\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    operating_margin = total_capacity - total_gen - total_reserve\n\n    # Build report\n    print(\"\\n4. Generating report...\")\n    report = {\n        'generator_dispatch': generator_dispatch,\n        'totals': {\n            'cost_dollars_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'load_MW': float(total_load),\n            'generation_MW': float(total_gen),\n            'reserve_MW': float(total_reserve)\n        },\n        'most_loaded_lines': [\n            {\n                'from': line['from'],\n                'to': line['to'],\n                'loading_pct': float(line['loading_pct'])\n            }\n            for line in most_loaded_lines\n        ],\n        'operating_margin_MW': float(operating_margin)\n    }\n\n    # Save report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"DISPATCH ANALYSIS COMPLETE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {total_gen:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Reserves: {total_reserve:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Operating Margin: {operating_margin:.2f} MW\")\n    print(\"\\nTop 3 Most Loaded Lines:\")\n    for line in most_loaded_lines:\n        print(f\"  {line['from']} -> {line['to']}: {line['loading_pct']:.1f}%\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nReport saved to /root/report.json\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Power balance: sum(P_g) = sum(loads)\n    2. Generator limits: Pmin <= P_g <= Pmax\n    3. Reserve limits: 0 <= R_g <= Pmax - P_g\n    4. Spinning reserve requirement: sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * sum(loads)\n    \"\"\"\n    n_gen = net['n_gen']\n\n    # For linear programming, we'll use linear costs\n    # Convert quadratic costs to linear by linearizing around an operating point\n    c_linear = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c2, c1, c0 = net['gen_costs'][i, 2], net['gen_costs'][i, 1], net['gen_costs'][i, 0]\n        # Marginal cost = 2*c2*P + c1, use mid-point as starting point\n        p_mid = (net['gen_pmax'][i] + net['gen_pmin'][i]) / 2\n        marginal_cost = 2 * c2 * p_mid + c1\n        c_linear.append(marginal_cost)\n\n    # Variables: [P_g[0], ..., P_g[n-1], R_g[0], ..., R_g[n-1]]\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen\n\n    # Objective function coefficients\n    c_obj = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c_obj[i] = c_linear[i]  # Cost for P_g\n        c_obj[i + n_gen] = c_linear[i] * 0.1  # Cost for reserves (10% of energy cost)\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraint (system-wide)\n    # sum(P_g) - sum(loads) = 0\n    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_eq = np.array([np.sum(net['loads'])])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_eq[0, i] = 1.0  # P_g contributes to power balance\n\n    # 2. Generator Pmin/Pmax constraints\n    # P_g >= Pmin\n    A_ub_pmin = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmin = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmin[i, i] = 1.0  # P_g >= Pmin\n        b_ub_pmin[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # P_g <= Pmax (converted to -P_g >= -Pmax)\n    A_ub_pmax = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmax = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmax[i, i] = -1.0  # -P_g >= -Pmax\n        b_ub_pmax[i] = -net['gen_pmin'][i]\n\n    # 3. Reserve constraints\n    # R_g >= 0\n    A_ub_r_min = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_min[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0\n        b_ub_r_min[i] = 0\n\n    # R_g <= Pmax - P_g  (converted to P_g + R_g <= Pmax)\n    A_ub_r_max = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i] = 1.0  # +P_g\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0  # +R_g\n        b_ub_r_max[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 4. Spinning reserve requirement\n    # sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    min_reserves = RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n\n    A_ub_res = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_ub_res = np.array([min_reserves])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_res[0, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # -sum(R_g) >= -min_reserves\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A_ub = np.vstack([A_ub_pmin, A_ub_pmax, A_ub_r_min, A_ub_r_max, A_ub_res])\n    b_ub = np.hstack([b_ub_pmin, b_ub_pmax, b_ub_r_min, b_ub_r_max, b_ub_res])\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    bounds = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((net['gen_pmin'][i], net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # P_g bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((0, net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # R_g bounds\n\n    return c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":171,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":704,"cache_read_input_tokens":37511},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5ad87fd2-00d1-4f4c-bf43-5781b0a712cc","uuid":"ed7e815d-de31-4256-989c-2baaa0844d89"}

Spinning reserve requirement: sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * sum(loads)\n    \"\"\"\n    n_gen = net['n_gen']\n\n    # For linear programming, we'll use linear costs\n    # Convert quadratic costs to linear by linearizing around an operating point\n    c_linear = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c2, c1, c0 = net['gen_costs'][i, 2], net['gen_costs'][i, 1], net['gen_costs'][i, 0]\n        # Marginal cost = 2*c2*P + c1, use mid-point as starting point\n        p_mid = (net['gen_pmax'][i] + net['gen_pmin'][i]) / 2\n        marginal_cost = 2 * c2 * p_mid + c1\n        c_linear.append(marginal_cost)\n\n    # Variables: [P_g[0], ..., P_g[n-1], R_g[0], ..., R_g[n-1]]\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen\n\n    # Objective function coefficients\n    c_obj = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c_obj[i] = c_linear[i]  # Cost for P_g\n        c_obj[i + n_gen] = c_linear[i] * 0.1  # Cost for reserves (10% of energy cost)\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraint (system-wide)\n    # sum(P_g) - sum(loads) = 0\n    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_eq = np.array([np.sum(net['loads'])])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_eq[0, i] = 1.0  # P_g contributes to power balance\n\n    # 2. Generator Pmin/Pmax constraints\n    # P_g >= Pmin\n    A_ub_pmin = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmin = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmin[i, i] = 1.0  # P_g >= Pmin\n        b_ub_pmin[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # P_g <= Pmax (converted to -P_g >= -Pmax)\n    A_ub_pmax = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmax = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmax[i, i] = -1.0  # -P_g >= -Pmax\n        b_ub_pmax[i] = -net['gen_pmin'][i]\n\n    # 3. Reserve constraints\n    # R_g >= 0\n    A_ub_r_min = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_min[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0\n        b_ub_r_min[i] = 0\n\n    # R_g <= Pmax - P_g  (converted to P_g + R_g <= Pmax)\n    A_ub_r_max = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i] = 1.0  # +P_g\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0  # +R_g\n        b_ub_r_max[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 4. Spinning reserve requirement\n    # sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    min_reserves = RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n\n    A_ub_res = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_ub_res = np.array([min_reserves])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_res[0, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # -sum(R_g) >= -min_reserves\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A_ub = np.vstack([A_ub_pmin, A_ub_pmax, A_ub_r_min, A_ub_r_max, A_ub_res])\n    b_ub = np.hstack([b_ub_pmin, b_ub_pmax, b_ub_r_min, b_ub_r_max, b_ub_res])\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    bounds = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((net['gen_pmin'][i], net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # P_g bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((0, net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # R_g bounds\n\n    return c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nGrid Dispatch Economic Dispatch Solver\nSolves DC power flow with economic dispatch and spinning reserves\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import linprog\nfrom scipy.sparse import csr_matrix\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Constants\nBASE_MVA = 100.0\nRESERVE_FRACTION = 0.05  # 5% spinning reserve requirement\nTOLERANCE = 1e-6\n\ndef parse_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Parse MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    base_mva = data['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    generators = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Extract bus data (MATPOWER format: BUS_I, BUS_TYPE, PD, QD, GS, BS, BUS_AREA, VM, VA, BASE_KV, ZONE, VMAX, VMIN)\n    bus_types = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1 = PQ, 2 = PV, 3 = Slack\n    loads = buses[:, 2].astype(float) / BASE_MVA * base_mva  # Power balance: sum(P_g) - sum(loads) - losses = 0\n    2. Generator limits: Pmin <= P_g <= Pmax\n    3. Reserve limits: 0 <= R_g <= min(Pmax - P_g, reserve_cap)\n    4. Power balance constraint\n    # Remove slack bus row from B matrix for power balance\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B, slack_bus, axis=0)\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B_reduced, slack_bus, axis=1)\n\n    # Get generator incidence matrix (which generators connect to which buses)\n    # For power balance at each bus: sum of P_g at bus - load - sum of flows = 0\n    A_eq = np.zeros((n_bus - 1, n_vars))\n    b_eq = np.zeros(n_bus - 1)\n\n    # Build power balance constraints\n    for bus_idx in range(n_bus):\n        if bus_idx == slack_bus:\n            continue\n\n        row_idx = bus_idx if bus_idx < slack_bus else bus_idx - 1\n\n        # Generators at this bus\n        for gen_idx, gen_bus in enumerate(net['gen_buses']):\n            if gen_bus - 1 == bus_idx:\n                A_eq[row_idx, gen_idx] = 1.0  # P_g contributes to power balance\n\n        # Load at this bus\n        b_eq[row_idx] = net['loads'][bus_idx]\n\n    # 2. Reserve constraints\n    # R_g >= 0\n    A_ub_r_min = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_min[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0\n        b_ub_r_min[i] = 0\n\n    # R_g <= Pmax - P_g\n    A_ub_r_max = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i] = -1.0  # -P_g\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0  # +R_g\n        b_ub_r_max[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 4. Spinning reserve requirement\n    # sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    min_reserves = RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n\n    A_ub_res = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_ub_res = np.array([min_reserves])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_res[0, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # Variable bounds\n    bounds = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((net['gen_pmin'][i], net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # P_g bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((0, net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # Build generator injection vector\n    P_gen_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(net['gen_buses']):\n        P_gen_bus[int(bus) - 1] += p_gen[i]\n\n    # Build load vector\n    P_load_bus = net['loads']\n\n    # Net injection at each bus\n    P_net = P_gen_bus - P_load_bus\n\n    # Solve DC power flow: B * theta = P_net (excluding slack)\n    B = net['B']\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B, slack_bus, axis=0)\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B_reduced, slack_bus, axis=1)\n\n    P_net_reduced = np.delete(P_net, slack_bus)\n\n    # Solve for voltage angles\n    theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_net_reduced)\n    theta = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta[np.arange(n_bus) != slack_bus] = theta_reduced\n    theta[slack_bus] = 0\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    flows = []\n    from_bus = net['from_bus']\n    to_bus = net['to_bus']\n    rate_a = net['rate_a']\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus:\n            flow = (theta[int(from_bus[i])] - theta[int(to_bus[i])]) / x[i]\n            flow_mw = flow * net['base_mva']\n            loading_pct = abs(flow_mw / rate_a[i]) * 100 if rate_a[i] > 0 else 0\n\n            flows.append({\n                'from': int(from_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'to': int(to_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'flow_MW': flow_mw,\n                'rating_MVA': rate_a[i],\n                'loading_pct': loading_pct\n            })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting Grid Dispatch Economic Dispatch Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # PMAX is at index 8, PMIN at 9)\n    gen_pmax = generators[:, 8].astype(float)\n    gen_pmin = generators[:, 9].astype(float)\n    gen_status = generators[:, 6].astype(int)\n\n    # Generator cost data (MATPOWER: MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, COST1, ..., COSTN)\n    # For MODEL=2 (polynomial), COSTS are: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    gen_costs = []\n    for i, gc in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(gc[0])\n        ncost = int(gc[3])\n        if model == 2 and ncost >= 3:\n            c2 = gc[4] if ncost > 3 else 0\n            c1 = gc[5] if ncost > 4 else gc[4]\n            c0 = gc[6] if ncost > 5 else gc[5]\n        elif model == 2 and ncost == 2:\n            c1 = gc[4]\n            c0 = gc[5]\n            c2 = 0\n        elif model == 2 and ncost == 1:\n            c0 = gc[4]\n            c1 = c2 = 0\n        else:\n            c1 = c2 = c0 = 0\n        gen_costs.append([c0, c1, c2])\n\n    gen_costs = np.array(gen_costs)\n\n    # Branch data (MATPOWER: F_BUS, T_BUS, BR_R, BR_X, BR_B, RATE_A, RATE_B, RATE_C, ...)\n    from_bus = (branches[:, 0] - 1).astype(int)\n    to_bus = (branches[:, 1] - 1).astype(int)\n    r = branches[:, 2].astype(float)\n    x = branches[:, 3].astype(float)\n    rate_a = branches[:, 5].astype(float)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus:\n            if x[i] != 0:\n                y = 1.0 / (r[i] + 1j * x[i])\n                Y[from_bus[i], from_bus[i]] += y\n                Y[to_bus[i], to_bus[i]] += y\n                Y[from_bus[i], to_bus[i]] -= y\n                Y[to_bus[i], from_bus[i]] -= y\n\n    # Build B matrix (susceptance matrix for DC approximation)\n    B = np.imag(Y)\n\n    # Find slack bus\n    slack_bus = np.where(bus_types == 3)[0]\n    if len(slack_bus) == 0:\n        slack_bus = 0\n    else:\n        slack_bus = slack_bus[0]\n\n    return {\n        'base_mva': base_mva,\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'bus_types': bus_types,\n        'slack_bus': slack_bus,\n        'loads': loads,\n        'gen_buses': gen_buses,\n        'gen_pmax': gen_pmax,\n        'gen_pmin': gen_pmin,\n        'gen_status': gen_status,\n        'gen_costs': gen_costs,\n        'from_bus': from_bus,\n        'to_bus': to_bus,\n        'rate_a': rate_a,\n        'B': B\n    }\n\ndef build_dispatch_problem(net):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build economic dispatch optimization problem\n\n    Variables:\n    - P_g[i] for each generator i (real power output)\n    - R_g[i] for each generator i (spinning reserves)\n\n    Objective: Minimize generation cost\n    Minimize sum(c2_i * P_g[i]^2 + c1_i * P_g[i] + c0_i + reserve_cost_factor * R_g[i])\n\n    Constraints:\n    1. Power balance: sum(P_g) = sum(loads)\n    2. Generator limits: Pmin <= P_g <= Pmax\n    3. Reserve limits: 0 <= R_g <= Pmax - P_g\n    4. Spinning reserve requirement: sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * sum(loads)\n    \"\"\"\n    n_gen = net['n_gen']\n\n    # For linear programming, we'll use linear costs\n    # Convert quadratic costs to linear by linearizing around an operating point\n    c_linear = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c2, c1, c0 = net['gen_costs'][i, 2], net['gen_costs'][i, 1], net['gen_costs'][i, 0]\n        # Marginal cost = 2*c2*P + c1, use mid-point as starting point\n        p_mid = (net['gen_pmax'][i] + net['gen_pmin'][i]) / 2\n        marginal_cost = 2 * c2 * p_mid + c1\n        c_linear.append(marginal_cost)\n\n    # Variables: [P_g[0], ..., P_g[n-1], R_g[0], ..., R_g[n-1]]\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen\n\n    # Objective function coefficients\n    c_obj = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c_obj[i] = c_linear[i]  # Cost for P_g\n        c_obj[i + n_gen] = c_linear[i] * 0.1  # Cost for reserves (10% of energy cost)\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraint (system-wide)\n    # sum(P_g) - sum(loads) = 0\n    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_eq = np.array([np.sum(net['loads'])])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_eq[0, i] = 1.0  # P_g contributes to power balance\n\n    # 2. Generator Pmin/Pmax constraints\n    # P_g >= Pmin\n    A_ub_pmin = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmin = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmin[i, i] = 1.0  # P_g >= Pmin\n        b_ub_pmin[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # P_g <= Pmax (converted to -P_g >= -Pmax)\n    A_ub_pmax = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmax = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmax[i, i] = -1.0  # -P_g >= -Pmax\n        b_ub_pmax[i] = -net['gen_pmin'][i]\n\n    # 3. Reserve constraints\n    # R_g >= 0\n    A_ub_r_min = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_min[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0\n        b_ub_r_min[i] = 0\n\n    # R_g <= Pmax - P_g  (converted to P_g + R_g <= Pmax)\n    A_ub_r_max = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i] = 1.0  # +P_g\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0  # +R_g\n        b_ub_r_max[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 4. Spinning reserve requirement\n    # sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    min_reserves = RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n\n    A_ub_res = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_ub_res = np.array([min_reserves])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_res[0, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # -sum(R_g) >= -min_reserves\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A_ub = np.vstack([A_ub_pmin, A_ub_pmax, A_ub_r_min, A_ub_r_max, A_ub_res])\n    b_ub = np.hstack([b_ub_pmin, b_ub_pmax, b_ub_r_min, b_ub_r_max, b_ub_res])\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    bounds = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((net['gen_pmin'][i], net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # P_g bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((0, net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # R_g bounds\n\n    return c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds\n\ndef solve_dispatch(net):\n    \"\"\"Solve the economic dispatch problem\"\"\"\n    print(\"Building optimization problem...\")\n    c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds = build_dispatch_problem(net)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {len(c_obj)} variables, {len(b_ub)} inequality constraints, {len(b_eq)} equality constraints\")\n\n    print(\"Solving optimization problem...\")\n    # Use linprog with sparse matrices for efficiency\n    result = linprog(\n        c=c_obj,\n        A_ub=A_ub if A_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        b_ub=b_ub if b_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        A_eq=A_eq if A_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        b_eq=b_eq if b_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        bounds=bounds,\n        method='highs',\n        options={'disp': True}\n    )\n\n    if result.success:\n        print(\"Optimization successful!\")\n        p_gen = result.x[:net['n_gen']]\n        r_gen = result.x[net['n_gen']:]\n\n        return p_gen, r_gen, result\n    else:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n        return None, None, result\n\ndef calculate_line_flows(net, p_gen):\n    \"\"\"Calculate DC power flow and line loading\"\"\"\n    n_bus = net['n_bus']\n    n_branch = net['n_branch']\n    slack_bus = net['slack_bus']\n\n    # Build generator injection vector\n    P_gen_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(net['gen_buses']):\n        P_gen_bus[int(bus) - 1] += p_gen[i]\n\n    # Build load vector\n    P_load_bus = net['loads']\n\n    # Net injection at each bus\n    P_net = P_gen_bus - P_load_bus\n\n    # Solve DC power flow: B * theta = P_net (excluding slack)\n    B = net['B']\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B, slack_bus, axis=0)\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B_reduced, slack_bus, axis=1)\n\n    P_net_reduced = np.delete(P_net, slack_bus)\n\n    # Solve for voltage angles\n    theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_net_reduced)\n    theta = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta[np.arange(n_bus) != slack_bus] = theta_reduced\n    theta[slack_bus] = 0\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    flows = []\n    from_bus = net['from_bus']\n    to_bus = net['to_bus']\n    rate_a = net['rate_a']\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus:\n            flow = (theta[int(from_bus[i])] - theta[int(to_bus[i])]) / x[i]\n            flow_mw = flow * net['base_mva']\n            loading_pct = abs(flow_mw / rate_a[i]) * 100 if rate_a[i] > 0 else 0\n\n            flows.append({\n                'from': int(from_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'to': int(to_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'flow_MW': flow_mw,\n                'rating_MVA': rate_a[i],\n                'loading_pct': loading_pct\n            })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting Grid Dispatch Economic Dispatch Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"\\n1. Loading network data...\")\n    net = parse_network('/root/network.json')\n    print(f\"   - Buses: {net['n_bus']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Generators: {net['n_gen']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Transmission lines: {net['n_branch']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Total load: {np.sum(net['loads']):.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Solve dispatch\n    print(\"\\n2. Solving economic dispatch...\")\n    p_gen, r_gen, result = solve_dispatch(net)\n\n    if p_gen is None:\n        print(\"ERROR: Dispatch solution failed\")\n        return\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    print(\"\\n3. Calculating transmission line flows...\")\n    flows = calculate_line_flows(net, p_gen)\n\n    # Prepare results\n    generator_dispatch = []\n    total_cost = 0\n    total_gen = 0\n    total_reserve = 0\n\n    for i in range(net['n_gen']):\n        bus_id = int(net['gen_buses'][i])\n        pmax = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n        p_min = net['gen_pmin'][i]\n        output_mw = p_gen[i]\n        reserve_mw = r_gen[i]\n\n        # Calculate cost (using actual quadratic cost)\n        c0, c1, c2 = net['gen_costs'][i]\n        cost = c2 * output_mw**2 + c1 * output_mw + c0\n\n        total_cost += cost\n        total_gen += output_mw\n        total_reserve += reserve_mw\n\n        generator_dispatch.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'output_MW': float(output_mw),\n            'reserve_MW': float(reserve_mw),\n            'pmax_MW': float(pmax)\n        })\n\n    # Sort lines by loading\n    flows_sorted = sorted(flows, key=lambda x: abs(x['loading_pct']), reverse=True)\n    most_loaded_lines = flows_sorted[:3]\n\n    # Calculate operating margin\n    total_capacity = np.sum(net['gen_pmax'])\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    operating_margin = total_capacity - total_gen - total_reserve\n\n    # Build report\n    print(\"\\n4. Generating report...\")\n    report = {\n        'generator_dispatch': generator_dispatch,\n        'totals': {\n            'cost_dollars_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'load_MW': float(total_load),\n            'generation_MW': float(total_gen),\n            'reserve_MW': float(total_reserve)\n        },\n        'most_loaded_lines': [\n            {\n                'from': line['from'],\n                'to': line['to'],\n                'loading_pct': float(line['loading_pct'])\n            }\n            for line in most_loaded_lines\n        ],\n        'operating_margin_MW': float(operating_margin)\n    }\n\n    # Save report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"DISPATCH ANALYSIS COMPLETE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {total_gen:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Reserves: {total_reserve:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Operating Margin: {operating_margin:.2f} MW\")\n    print(\"\\nTop 3 Most Loaded Lines:\")\n    for line in most_loaded_lines:\n        print(f\"  {line['from']} -> {line['to']}: {line['loading_pct']:.1f}%\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nReport saved to /root/report.json\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":274,"oldLines":9,"newStart":274,"newLines":11,"lines":["     from_bus = net['from_bus']","     to_bus = net['to_bus']","     rate_a = net['rate_a']","+    r = net.get('r', np.zeros(n_branch))","+    x = net.get('x', np.zeros(n_branch))"," ","     for i in range(n_branch):","-        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus:","+        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus and x[i] != 0:","             flow = (theta[int(from_bus[i])] - PMAX is at index 8, PMIN at 9)\n    gen_pmax = generators[:, 8].astype(float)\n    gen_pmin = generators[:, 9].astype(float)\n    gen_status = generators[:, 6].astype(int)\n\n    # Generator cost data (MATPOWER: MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, COST1, ..., COSTN)\n    # For MODEL=2 (polynomial), COSTS are: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    gen_costs = []\n    for i, gc in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(gc[0])\n        ncost = int(gc[3])\n        if model == 2 and ncost >= 3:\n            c2 = gc[4] if ncost > 3 else 0\n            c1 = gc[5] if ncost > 4 else gc[4]\n            c0 = gc[6] if ncost > 5 else gc[5]\n        elif model == 2 and ncost == 2:\n            c1 = gc[4]\n            c0 = gc[5]\n            c2 = 0\n        elif model == 2 and ncost == 1:\n            c0 = gc[4]\n            c1 = c2 = 0\n        else:\n            c1 = c2 = c0 = 0\n        gen_costs.append([c0, c1, c2])\n\n    gen_costs = np.array(gen_costs)\n\n    # Branch data (MATPOWER: F_BUS, T_BUS, BR_R, BR_X, BR_B, RATE_A, RATE_B, RATE_C, ...)\n    from_bus = (branches[:, 0] - 1).astype(int)\n    to_bus = (branches[:, 1] - 1).astype(int)\n    r = branches[:, 2].astype(float)\n    x = branches[:, 3].astype(float)\n    rate_a = branches[:, 5].astype(float)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus:\n            if x[i] != 0:\n                y = 1.0 / (r[i] + 1j * x[i])\n                Y[from_bus[i], from_bus[i]] += y\n                Y[to_bus[i], to_bus[i]] += y\n                Y[from_bus[i], to_bus[i]] -= y\n                Y[to_bus[i], from_bus[i]] -= y\n\n    # Build B matrix (susceptance matrix for DC approximation)\n    B = np.imag(Y)\n\n    # Find slack bus\n    slack_bus = np.where(bus_types == 3)[0]\n    if len(slack_bus) == 0:\n        slack_bus = 0\n    else:\n        slack_bus = slack_bus[0]\n\n    return {\n        'base_mva': base_mva,\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'bus_types': bus_types,\n        'slack_bus': slack_bus,\n        'loads': loads,\n        'gen_buses': gen_buses,\n        'gen_pmax': gen_pmax,\n        'gen_pmin': gen_pmin,\n        'gen_status': gen_status,\n        'gen_costs': gen_costs,\n        'from_bus': from_bus,\n        'to_bus': to_bus,\n        'rate_a': rate_a,\n        'B': B\n    }\n\ndef build_dispatch_problem(net):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build economic dispatch optimization problem\n\n    Variables:\n    - P_g[i] for each generator i (real power output)\n    - R_g[i] for each generator i (spinning reserves)\n\n    Objective: Minimize generation cost\n    Minimize sum(c2_i * P_g[i]^2 + c1_i * P_g[i] + c0_i + reserve_cost_factor * R_g[i])\n\n    Constraints:\n    1. Power balance: sum(P_g) = sum(loads)\n    2. Generator limits: Pmin <= P_g <= Pmax\n    3. Reserve limits: 0 <= R_g <= Pmax - P_g\n    4. Spinning reserve requirement: sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * sum(loads)\n    \"\"\"\n    n_gen = net['n_gen']\n\n    # For linear programming, we'll use linear costs\n    # Convert quadratic costs to linear by linearizing around an operating point\n    c_linear = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c2, c1, c0 = net['gen_costs'][i, 2], net['gen_costs'][i, 1], net['gen_costs'][i, 0]\n        # Marginal cost = 2*c2*P + c1, use mid-point as starting point\n        p_mid = (net['gen_pmax'][i] + net['gen_pmin'][i]) / 2\n        marginal_cost = 2 * c2 * p_mid + c1\n        c_linear.append(marginal_cost)\n\n    # Variables: [P_g[0], ..., P_g[n-1], R_g[0], ..., R_g[n-1]]\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen\n\n    # Objective function coefficients\n    c_obj = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c_obj[i] = c_linear[i]  # Cost for P_g\n        c_obj[i + n_gen] = c_linear[i] * 0.1  # Cost for reserves (10% of energy cost)\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraint (system-wide)\n    # sum(P_g) - sum(loads) = 0\n    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_eq = np.array([np.sum(net['loads'])])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_eq[0, i] = 1.0  # P_g contributes to power balance\n\n    # 2. Generator Pmin/Pmax constraints\n    # P_g >= Pmin\n    A_ub_pmin = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmin = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmin[i, i] = 1.0  # P_g >= Pmin\n        b_ub_pmin[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # P_g <= Pmax (converted to -P_g >= -Pmax)\n    A_ub_pmax = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmax = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmax[i, i] = -1.0  # -P_g >= -Pmax\n        b_ub_pmax[i] = -net['gen_pmin'][i]\n\n    # 3. Reserve constraints\n    # R_g >= 0\n    A_ub_r_min = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_min[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0\n        b_ub_r_min[i] = 0\n\n    # R_g <= Pmax - P_g  (converted to P_g + R_g <= Pmax)\n    A_ub_r_max = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i] = 1.0  # +P_g\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0  # +R_g\n        b_ub_r_max[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 4. Spinning reserve requirement\n    # sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    min_reserves = RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n\n    A_ub_res = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_ub_res = np.array([min_reserves])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_res[0, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # -sum(R_g) >= -min_reserves\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A_ub = np.vstack([A_ub_pmin, A_ub_pmax, A_ub_r_min, A_ub_r_max, A_ub_res])\n    b_ub = np.hstack([b_ub_pmin, b_ub_pmax, b_ub_r_min, b_ub_r_max, b_ub_res])\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    bounds = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((net['gen_pmin'][i], net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # P_g bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((0, net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # R_g bounds\n\n    return c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds\n\ndef solve_dispatch(net):\n    \"\"\"Solve the economic dispatch problem\"\"\"\n    print(\"Building optimization problem...\")\n    c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds = build_dispatch_problem(net)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {len(c_obj)} variables, {len(b_ub)} inequality constraints, {len(b_eq)} equality constraints\")\n\n    print(\"Solving optimization problem...\")\n    # Use linprog with sparse matrices for efficiency\n    result = linprog(\n        c=c_obj,\n        A_ub=A_ub if A_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        b_ub=b_ub if b_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        A_eq=A_eq if A_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        b_eq=b_eq if b_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        bounds=bounds,\n        method='highs',\n        options={'disp': True}\n    )\n\n    if result.success:\n        print(\"Optimization successful!\")\n        p_gen = result.x[:net['n_gen']]\n        r_gen = result.x[net['n_gen']:]\n\n        return p_gen, r_gen, result\n    else:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n        return None, None, result\n\ndef calculate_line_flows(net, p_gen):\n    \"\"\"Calculate DC power flow and line loading\"\"\"\n    n_bus = net['n_bus']\n    n_branch = net['n_branch']\n    slack_bus = net['slack_bus']\n\n    # Build generator injection vector\n    P_gen_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(net['gen_buses']):\n        P_gen_bus[int(bus) - 1] += p_gen[i]\n\n    # Build load vector\n    P_load_bus = net['loads']\n\n    # Net injection at each bus\n    P_net = P_gen_bus - P_load_bus\n\n    # Solve DC power flow: B * theta = P_net (excluding slack)\n    B = net['B']\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B, slack_bus, axis=0)\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B_reduced, slack_bus, axis=1)\n\n    P_net_reduced = np.delete(P_net, slack_bus)\n\n    # Solve for voltage angles\n    theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_net_reduced)\n    theta = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta[np.arange(n_bus) != slack_bus] = theta_reduced\n    theta[slack_bus] = 0\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    flows = []\n    from_bus = net['from_bus']\n    to_bus = net['to_bus']\n    rate_a = net['rate_a']\n    r = net.get('r', np.zeros(n_branch))\n    x = net.get('x', np.zeros(n_branch))\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus and x[i] != 0:\n            flow = (theta[int(from_bus[i])] - theta[int(to_bus[i])]) / x[i]\n            flow_mw = flow * net['base_mva']\n            loading_pct = abs(flow_mw / rate_a[i]) * 100 if rate_a[i] > 0 else 0\n\n            flows.append({\n                'from': int(from_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'to': int(to_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'flow_MW': flow_mw,\n                'rating_MVA': rate_a[i],\n                'loading_pct': loading_pct\n            })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting Grid Dispatch Economic Dispatch Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"\\n1. Loading network data...\")\n    net = parse_network('/root/network.json')\n    print(f\"   - Buses: {net['n_bus']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Generators: {net['n_gen']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Transmission lines: {net['n_branch']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Total load: {np.sum(net['loads']):.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Solve dispatch\n    print(\"\\n2. Solving economic dispatch...\")\n    p_gen, r_gen, result = solve_dispatch(net)\n\n    if p_gen is None:\n        print(\"ERROR: Dispatch solution failed\")\n        return\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    print(\"\\n3. Calculating transmission line flows...\")\n    flows = calculate_line_flows(net, p_gen)\n\n    # Prepare results\n    generator_dispatch = []\n    total_cost = 0\n    total_gen = 0\n    total_reserve = 0\n\n    for i in range(net['n_gen']):\n        bus_id = int(net['gen_buses'][i])\n        pmax = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n        p_min = net['gen_pmin'][i]\n        output_mw = p_gen[i]\n        reserve_mw = r_gen[i]\n\n        # Calculate cost (using actual quadratic cost)\n        c0, c1, c2 = net['gen_costs'][i]\n        cost = c2 * output_mw**2 + c1 * output_mw + c0\n\n        total_cost += cost\n        total_gen += output_mw\n        total_reserve += reserve_mw\n\n        generator_dispatch.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'output_MW': float(output_mw),\n            'reserve_MW': float(reserve_mw),\n            'pmax_MW': float(pmax)\n        })\n\n    # Sort lines by loading\n    flows_sorted = sorted(flows, key=lambda x: abs(x['loading_pct']), reverse=True)\n    most_loaded_lines = flows_sorted[:3]\n\n    # Calculate operating margin\n    total_capacity = np.sum(net['gen_pmax'])\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    operating_margin = total_capacity - total_gen - total_reserve\n\n    # Build report\n    print(\"\\n4. Generating report...\")\n    report = {\n        'generator_dispatch': generator_dispatch,\n        'totals': {\n            'cost_dollars_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'load_MW': float(total_load),\n            'generation_MW': float(total_gen),\n            'reserve_MW': float(total_reserve)\n        },\n        'most_loaded_lines': [\n            {\n                'from': line['from'],\n                'to': line['to'],\n                'loading_pct': float(line['loading_pct'])\n            }\n            for line in most_loaded_lines\n        ],\n        'operating_margin_MW': float(operating_margin)\n    }\n\n    # Save report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"DISPATCH ANALYSIS COMPLETE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {total_gen:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Reserves: {total_reserve:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Operating Margin: {operating_margin:.2f} MW\")\n    print(\"\\nTop 3 Most Loaded Lines:\")\n    for line in most_loaded_lines:\n        print(f\"  {line['from']} -> {line['to']}: {line['loading_pct']:.1f}%\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nReport saved to /root/report.json\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":156,"oldLines":26,"newStart":156,"newLines":26,"lines":["         A_eq[0, i] = 1.0  # P_g contributes to power balance"," ","     # 2. 5% spinning reserve requirement\nTOLERANCE = 1e-6\n\ndef parse_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Parse MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    base_mva = data['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    generators = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # P_g[i] for each generator i (real power output)\n    - R_g[i] for each generator i (spinning reserves)\n\n    Objective: Minimize generation cost\n    Minimize sum(c2_i * P_g[i]^2 + c1_i * P_g[i] + c0_i + reserve_cost_factor * R_g[i])\n\n    Constraints:\n    1. Power balance: sum(P_g) = sum(loads)\n    2. Generator limits: Pmin <= P_g <= Pmax\n    3. Reserve limits: 0 <= R_g <= Pmax - P_g\n    4. Spinning reserve requirement: sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * sum(loads)\n    \"\"\"\n    n_gen = net['n_gen']\n\n    # For linear programming, we'll use linear costs\n    # Convert quadratic costs to linear by linearizing around an operating point\n    c_linear = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c2, c1, c0 = net['gen_costs'][i, 2], net['gen_costs'][i, 1], net['gen_costs'][i, 0]\n        # Marginal cost = 2*c2*P + c1, use mid-point as starting point\n        p_mid = (net['gen_pmax'][i] + net['gen_pmin'][i]) / 2\n        marginal_cost = 2 * c2 * p_mid + c1\n        c_linear.append(marginal_cost)\n\n    # Variables: [P_g[0], ..., P_g[n-1], R_g[0], ..., R_g[n-1]]\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen\n\n    # Objective function coefficients\n    c_obj = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c_obj[i] = c_linear[i]  # Cost for P_g\n        c_obj[i + n_gen] = c_linear[i] * 0.1  # Cost for reserves (10% of energy cost)\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraint (system-wide)\n    # sum(P_g) - sum(loads) = 0\n    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_eq = np.array([np.sum(net['loads'])])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_eq[0, i] = 1.0  # P_g contributes to power balance\n\n    # 2. Generator Pmin/Pmax constraints\n    # P_g >= Pmin (converted to -P_g <= -Pmin)\n    A_ub_pmin = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmin = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmin[i, i] = -1.0  # -P_g <= -Pmin\n        b_ub_pmin[i] = -net['gen_pmin'][i]\n\n    # P_g <= Pmax\n    A_ub_pmax = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmax = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmax[i, i] = 1.0  # P_g <= Pmax\n        b_ub_pmax[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 3. Reserve constraints\n    # R_g >= 0 (converted to -R_g <= 0)\n    A_ub_r_min = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_min[i, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # -R_g <= 0\n        b_ub_r_min[i] = 0\n\n    # R_g <= Pmax - P_g  (converted to P_g + R_g <= Pmax)\n    A_ub_r_max = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i] = 1.0  # +P_g\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0  # +R_g\n        b_ub_r_max[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 4. Spinning reserve requirement\n    # sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    min_reserves = RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n\n    A_ub_res = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_ub_res = np.array([min_reserves])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_res[0, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # -sum(R_g) >= -min_reserves\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A_ub = np.vstack([A_ub_pmin, A_ub_pmax, A_ub_r_min, A_ub_r_max, A_ub_res])\n    b_ub = np.hstack([b_ub_pmin, b_ub_pmax, b_ub_r_min, b_ub_r_max, b_ub_res])\n\n    # R_g bounds\n\n    return c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds\n\ndef solve_dispatch(net):\n    \"\"\"Solve the economic dispatch problem\"\"\"\n    print(\"Building optimization problem...\")\n    c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds = build_dispatch_problem(net)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {len(c_obj)} variables, {len(b_ub)} inequality constraints, {len(b_eq)} equality constraints\")\n\n    print(\"Solving optimization problem...\")\n    # Use linprog with sparse matrices for efficiency\n    result = linprog(\n        c=c_obj,\n        A_ub=A_ub if A_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        b_ub=b_ub if b_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        A_eq=A_eq if A_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        b_eq=b_eq if b_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        bounds=bounds,\n        method='highs',\n        options={'disp': True}\n    )\n\n    if result.success:\n        print(\"Optimization successful!\")\n        p_gen = result.x[:net['n_gen']]\n        r_gen = result.x[net['n_gen']:]\n\n        return p_gen, r_gen, result\n    else:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n        return None, None, result\n\ndef calculate_line_flows(net, p_gen):\n    \"\"\"Calculate DC power flow and line loading\"\"\"\n    n_bus = net['n_bus']\n    n_branch = net['n_branch']\n    slack_bus = net['slack_bus']\n\n    # 5% spinning reserve requirement\nTOLERANCE = 1e-6\n\ndef parse_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Parse MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    base_mva = data['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    generators = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Extract bus data (MATPOWER format: BUS_I, BUS_TYPE, PD, QD, GS, BS, BUS_AREA, VM, VA, BASE_KV, ZONE, VMAX, VMIN)\n    bus_types = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1 = PQ, 2 = PV, 3 = Slack\n    loads = buses[:, 2].astype(float) / BASE_MVA * base_mva  # Convert to MW\n    gen_buses = generators[:, 0].astype(int)\n\n    # Generator data (MATPOWER: BUS, PG, QG, QMAX, QMIN, VG, MBASE, STATUS, PMAX, PMIN, ... PMAX is at index 8, PMIN at 9)\n    gen_pmax = generators[:, 8].astype(float)\n    gen_pmin = generators[:, 9].astype(float)\n    gen_status = generators[:, 6].astype(int)\n\n    # Generator cost data (MATPOWER: MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, COST1, ..., COSTN)\n    # For MODEL=2 (polynomial), COSTS are: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    gen_costs = []\n    for i, gc in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(gc[0])\n        ncost = int(gc[3])\n        if model == 2 and ncost >= 3:\n            c2 = gc[4] if ncost > 3 else 0\n            c1 = gc[5] if ncost > 4 else gc[4]\n            c0 = gc[6] if ncost > 5 else gc[5]\n        elif model == 2 and ncost == 2:\n            c1 = gc[4]\n            c0 = gc[5]\n            c2 = 0\n        elif model == 2 and ncost == 1:\n            c0 = gc[4]\n            c1 = c2 = 0\n        else:\n            c1 = c2 = c0 = 0\n        gen_costs.append([c0, c1, c2])\n\n    gen_costs = np.array(gen_costs)\n\n    # Branch data (MATPOWER: F_BUS, T_BUS, BR_R, BR_X, BR_B, RATE_A, RATE_B, RATE_C, ...)\n    from_bus = (branches[:, 0] - 1).astype(int)\n    to_bus = (branches[:, 1] - 1).astype(int)\n    r = branches[:, 2].astype(float)\n    x = branches[:, 3].astype(float)\n    rate_a = branches[:, 5].astype(float)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus:\n            if x[i] != 0:\n                y = 1.0 / (r[i] + 1j * x[i])\n                Y[from_bus[i], from_bus[i]] += y\n                Y[to_bus[i], to_bus[i]] += y\n                Y[from_bus[i], to_bus[i]] -= y\n                Y[to_bus[i], from_bus[i]] -= y\n\n    # Build B matrix (susceptance matrix for DC approximation)\n    B = np.imag(Y)\n\n    # Find slack bus\n    slack_bus = np.where(bus_types == 3)[0]\n    if len(slack_bus) == 0:\n        slack_bus = 0\n    else:\n        slack_bus = slack_bus[0]\n\n    return {\n        'base_mva': base_mva,\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'bus_types': bus_types,\n        'slack_bus': slack_bus,\n        'loads': loads,\n        'gen_buses': gen_buses,\n        'gen_pmax': gen_pmax,\n        'gen_pmin': gen_pmin,\n        'gen_status': gen_status,\n        'gen_costs': gen_costs,\n        'from_bus': from_bus,\n        'to_bus': to_bus,\n        'rate_a': rate_a,\n        'B': B\n    }\n\ndef build_dispatch_problem(net):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build economic dispatch optimization problem\n\n    Variables:\n    - P_g[i] for each generator i (real power output)\n    - R_g[i] for each generator i (spinning reserves)\n\n    Objective: Minimize generation cost\n    Minimize sum(c2_i * P_g[i]^2 + c1_i * P_g[i] + c0_i + reserve_cost_factor * R_g[i])\n\n    Constraints:\n    1. Power balance: sum(P_g) = sum(loads)\n    2. Generator limits: Pmin <= P_g <= Pmax\n    3. Reserve limits: 0 <= R_g <= Pmax - P_g\n    4. Spinning reserve requirement: sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * sum(loads)\n    \"\"\"\n    n_gen = net['n_gen']\n\n    # For linear programming, we'll use linear costs\n    # Convert quadratic costs to linear by linearizing around an operating point\n    c_linear = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c2, c1, c0 = net['gen_costs'][i, 2], net['gen_costs'][i, 1], net['gen_costs'][i, 0]\n        # Marginal cost = 2*c2*P + c1, use mid-point as starting point\n        p_mid = (net['gen_pmax'][i] + net['gen_pmin'][i]) / 2\n        marginal_cost = 2 * c2 * p_mid + c1\n        c_linear.append(marginal_cost)\n\n    # Variables: [P_g[0], ..., P_g[n-1], R_g[0], ..., R_g[n-1]]\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen\n\n    # Objective function coefficients\n    c_obj = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c_obj[i] = c_linear[i]  # Cost for P_g\n        c_obj[i + n_gen] = c_linear[i] * 0.1  # Cost for reserves (10% of energy cost)\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraint (system-wide)\n    # sum(P_g) - sum(loads) = 0\n    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_eq = np.array([np.sum(net['loads'])])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_eq[0, i] = 1.0  # P_g contributes to power balance\n\n    # 2. Generator Pmin/Pmax constraints\n    # P_g >= Pmin (converted to -P_g <= -Pmin)\n    A_ub_pmin = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmin = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmin[i, i] = -1.0  # -P_g <= -Pmin\n        b_ub_pmin[i] = -net['gen_pmin'][i]\n\n    # P_g <= Pmax\n    A_ub_pmax = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmax = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmax[i, i] = 1.0  # P_g <= Pmax\n        b_ub_pmax[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 3. Reserve constraints\n    # R_g >= 0 (converted to -R_g <= 0)\n    A_ub_r_min = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_min[i, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # -R_g <= 0\n        b_ub_r_min[i] = 0\n\n    # R_g <= Pmax - P_g  (converted to P_g + R_g <= Pmax)\n    A_ub_r_max = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i] = 1.0  # +P_g\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0  # +R_g\n        b_ub_r_max[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 4. Spinning reserve requirement\n    # sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    min_reserves = RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n\n    A_ub_res = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_ub_res = np.array([min_reserves])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_res[0, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # -sum(R_g) >= -min_reserves\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A_ub = np.vstack([A_ub_pmin, A_ub_pmax, A_ub_r_min, A_ub_r_max, A_ub_res])\n    b_ub = np.hstack([b_ub_pmin, b_ub_pmax, b_ub_r_min, b_ub_r_max, b_ub_res])\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    bounds = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((net['gen_pmin'][i], net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # P_g bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((0, net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # R_g bounds\n\n    return c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds\n\ndef solve_dispatch(net):\n    \"\"\"Solve the economic dispatch problem\"\"\"\n    print(\"Building optimization problem...\")\n    c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds = build_dispatch_problem(net)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {len(c_obj)} variables, {len(b_ub)} inequality constraints, {len(b_eq)} equality constraints\")\n\n    # Debug: Check reserve requirement\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    min_reserves = RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n    print(f\"Total load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Minimum reserves required (5%): {min_reserves:.2f} MW\")\n\n    print(\"Solving optimization problem...\")\n    # Use linprog with sparse matrices for efficiency\n    result = linprog(\n        c=c_obj,\n        A_ub=A_ub if A_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        b_ub=b_ub if b_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        A_eq=A_eq if A_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        b_eq=b_eq if b_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        bounds=bounds,\n        method='highs',\n        options={'disp': False}\n    )\n\n    if result.success:\n        print(\"Optimization successful!\")\n        p_gen = result.x[:net['n_gen']]\n        r_gen = result.x[net['n_gen']:]\n\n        # Debug: Check reserves\n        total_reserves = np.sum(r_gen)\n        print(f\"Total reserves allocated: {total_reserves:.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"Number of generators with reserves > 0: {np.sum(r_gen > 0.1)}\")\n\n        return p_gen, r_gen, result\n    else:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n        return None, None, result\n\ndef calculate_line_flows(net, p_gen):\n    \"\"\"Calculate DC power flow and line loading\"\"\"\n    n_bus = net['n_bus']\n    n_branch = net['n_branch']\n    slack_bus = net['slack_bus']\n\n    # Build generator injection vector\n    P_gen_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(net['gen_buses']):\n        P_gen_bus[int(bus) - 1] += p_gen[i]\n\n    # Build load vector\n    P_load_bus = net['loads']\n\n    # Net injection at each bus\n    P_net = P_gen_bus - P_load_bus\n\n    # Solve DC power flow: B * theta = P_net (excluding slack)\n    B = net['B']\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B, slack_bus, axis=0)\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B_reduced, slack_bus, axis=1)\n\n    P_net_reduced = np.delete(P_net, slack_bus)\n\n    # Solve for voltage angles\n    theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_net_reduced)\n    theta = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta[np.arange(n_bus) != slack_bus] = theta_reduced\n    theta[slack_bus] = 0\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    flows = []\n    from_bus = net['from_bus']\n    to_bus = net['to_bus']\n    rate_a = net['rate_a']\n    r = net.get('r', np.zeros(n_branch))\n    x = net.get('x', np.zeros(n_branch))\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus and x[i] != 0:\n            flow = (theta[int(from_bus[i])] - theta[int(to_bus[i])]) / x[i]\n            flow_mw = flow * net['base_mva']\n            loading_pct = abs(flow_mw / rate_a[i]) * 100 if rate_a[i] > 0 else 0\n\n            flows.append({\n                'from': int(from_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'to': int(to_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'flow_MW': flow_mw,\n                'rating_MVA': rate_a[i],\n                'loading_pct': loading_pct\n            })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting Grid Dispatch Economic Dispatch Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"\\n1. Loading network data...\")\n    net = parse_network('/root/network.json')\n    print(f\"   - Buses: {net['n_bus']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Generators: {net['n_gen']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Transmission lines: {net['n_branch']}\")\n    print(f\"   - Total load: {np.sum(net['loads']):.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Solve dispatch\n    print(\"\\n2. Solving economic dispatch...\")\n    p_gen, r_gen, result = solve_dispatch(net)\n\n    if p_gen is None:\n        print(\"ERROR: Dispatch solution failed\")\n        return\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    print(\"\\n3. Calculating transmission line flows...\")\n    flows = calculate_line_flows(net, p_gen)\n\n    # Prepare results\n    generator_dispatch = []\n    total_cost = 0\n    total_gen = 0\n    total_reserve = 0\n\n    for i in range(net['n_gen']):\n        bus_id = int(net['gen_buses'][i])\n        pmax = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n        p_min = net['gen_pmin'][i]\n        output_mw = p_gen[i]\n        reserve_mw = r_gen[i]\n\n        # Calculate cost (using actual quadratic cost)\n        c0, c1, c2 = net['gen_costs'][i]\n        cost = c2 * output_mw**2 + c1 * output_mw + c0\n\n        total_cost += cost\n        total_gen += output_mw\n        total_reserve += reserve_mw\n\n        generator_dispatch.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'output_MW': float(output_mw),\n            'reserve_MW': float(reserve_mw),\n            'pmax_MW': float(pmax)\n        })\n\n    # Sort lines by loading\n    flows_sorted = sorted(flows, key=lambda x: abs(x['loading_pct']), reverse=True)\n    most_loaded_lines = flows_sorted[:3]\n\n    # Calculate operating margin\n    total_capacity = np.sum(net['gen_pmax'])\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    operating_margin = total_capacity - total_gen - total_reserve\n\n    # Build report\n    print(\"\\n4. Generating report...\")\n    report = {\n        'generator_dispatch': generator_dispatch,\n        'totals': {\n            'cost_dollars_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'load_MW': float(total_load),\n            'generation_MW': float(total_gen),\n            'reserve_MW': float(total_reserve)\n        },\n        'most_loaded_lines': [\n            {\n                'from': line['from'],\n                'to': line['to'],\n                'loading_pct': float(line['loading_pct'])\n            }\n            for line in most_loaded_lines\n        ],\n        'operating_margin_MW': float(operating_margin)\n    }\n\n    # 5% spinning reserve requirement\nTOLERANCE = 1e-6\n\ndef parse_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Parse MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    base_mva = data['baseMVA']\n    buses = np.array(data['bus'])\n    generators = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Extract bus data (MATPOWER format: BUS_I, BUS_TYPE, PD, QD, GS, BS, BUS_AREA, VM, VA, BASE_KV, ZONE, VMAX, VMIN)\n    bus_types = buses[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1 = PQ, 2 = PV, 3 = Slack\n    loads = buses[:, 2].astype(float) / BASE_MVA * base_mva  # Convert to MW\n    gen_buses = generators[:, 0].astype(int)\n\n    # Generator data (MATPOWER: BUS, PG, QG, QMAX, QMIN, VG, MBASE, STATUS, PMAX, PMIN, ... PMAX is at index 8, PMIN at 9)\n    gen_pmax = generators[:, 8].astype(float)\n    gen_pmin = generators[:, 9].astype(float)\n    gen_status = generators[:, 6].astype(int)\n\n    # Generator cost data (MATPOWER: MODEL, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, NCOST, COST1, ..., COSTN)\n    # For MODEL=2 (polynomial), COSTS are: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    gen_costs = []\n    for i, gc in enumerate(gencost):\n        model = int(gc[0])\n        ncost = int(gc[3])\n        if model == 2 and ncost >= 3:\n            c2 = gc[4] if ncost > 3 else 0\n            c1 = gc[5] if ncost > 4 else gc[4]\n            c0 = gc[6] if ncost > 5 else gc[5]\n        elif model == 2 and ncost == 2:\n            c1 = gc[4]\n            c0 = gc[5]\n            c2 = 0\n        elif model == 2 and ncost == 1:\n            c0 = gc[4]\n            c1 = c2 = 0\n        else:\n            c1 = c2 = c0 = 0\n        gen_costs.append([c0, c1, c2])\n\n    gen_costs = np.array(gen_costs)\n\n    # Branch data (MATPOWER: F_BUS, T_BUS, BR_R, BR_X, BR_B, RATE_A, RATE_B, RATE_C, ...)\n    from_bus = (branches[:, 0] - 1).astype(int)\n    to_bus = (branches[:, 1] - 1).astype(int)\n    r = branches[:, 2].astype(float)\n    x = branches[:, 3].astype(float)\n    rate_a = branches[:, 5].astype(float)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus:\n            if x[i] != 0:\n                y = 1.0 / (r[i] + 1j * x[i])\n                Y[from_bus[i], from_bus[i]] += y\n                Y[to_bus[i], to_bus[i]] += y\n                Y[from_bus[i], to_bus[i]] -= y\n                Y[to_bus[i], from_bus[i]] -= y\n\n    # Build B matrix (susceptance matrix for DC approximation)\n    B = np.imag(Y)\n\n    # Find slack bus\n    slack_bus = np.where(bus_types == 3)[0]\n    if len(slack_bus) == 0:\n        slack_bus = 0\n    else:\n        slack_bus = slack_bus[0]\n\n    return {\n        'base_mva': base_mva,\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'bus_types': bus_types,\n        'slack_bus': slack_bus,\n        'loads': loads,\n        'gen_buses': gen_buses,\n        'gen_pmax': gen_pmax,\n        'gen_pmin': gen_pmin,\n        'gen_status': gen_status,\n        'gen_costs': gen_costs,\n        'from_bus': from_bus,\n        'to_bus': to_bus,\n        'rate_a': rate_a,\n        'B': B\n    }\n\ndef build_dispatch_problem(net):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build economic dispatch optimization problem\n\n    Variables:\n    - P_g[i] for each generator i (real power output)\n    - R_g[i] for each generator i (spinning reserves)\n\n    Objective: Minimize generation cost\n    Minimize sum(c2_i * P_g[i]^2 + c1_i * P_g[i] + c0_i + reserve_cost_factor * R_g[i])\n\n    Constraints:\n    1. Power balance: sum(P_g) = sum(loads)\n    2. Generator limits: Pmin <= P_g <= Pmax\n    3. Reserve limits: 0 <= R_g <= Pmax - P_g\n    4. Spinning reserve requirement: sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * sum(loads)\n    \"\"\"\n    n_gen = net['n_gen']\n\n    # For linear programming, we'll use linear costs\n    # Convert quadratic costs to linear by linearizing around an operating point\n    c_linear = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c2, c1, c0 = net['gen_costs'][i, 2], net['gen_costs'][i, 1], net['gen_costs'][i, 0]\n        # Marginal cost = 2*c2*P + c1, use mid-point as starting point\n        p_mid = (net['gen_pmax'][i] + net['gen_pmin'][i]) / 2\n        marginal_cost = 2 * c2 * p_mid + c1\n        c_linear.append(marginal_cost)\n\n    # Variables: [P_g[0], ..., P_g[n-1], R_g[0], ..., R_g[n-1]]\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gen\n\n    # Objective function coefficients\n    c_obj = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        c_obj[i] = c_linear[i]  # Cost for P_g\n        c_obj[i + n_gen] = c_linear[i] * 0.1  # Cost for reserves (10% of energy cost)\n\n    # 1. Power balance constraint (system-wide)\n    # sum(P_g) - sum(loads) = 0\n    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_eq = np.array([np.sum(net['loads'])])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_eq[0, i] = 1.0  # P_g contributes to power balance\n\n    # 2. Generator Pmin/Pmax constraints\n    # P_g >= Pmin (converted to -P_g <= -Pmin)\n    A_ub_pmin = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmin = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmin[i, i] = -1.0  # -P_g <= -Pmin\n        b_ub_pmin[i] = -net['gen_pmin'][i]\n\n    # P_g <= Pmax\n    A_ub_pmax = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_pmax = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_pmax[i, i] = 1.0  # P_g <= Pmax\n        b_ub_pmax[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 3. Reserve constraints\n    # R_g >= 0 (converted to -R_g <= 0)\n    A_ub_r_min = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_min = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_min[i, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # -R_g <= 0\n        b_ub_r_min[i] = 0\n\n    # R_g <= Pmax - P_g  (converted to P_g + R_g <= Pmax)\n    A_ub_r_max = np.zeros((n_gen, n_vars))\n    b_ub_r_max = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i] = 1.0  # +P_g\n        A_ub_r_max[i, i + n_gen] = 1.0  # +R_g\n        b_ub_r_max[i] = net['gen_pmax'][i]\n\n    # 4. Spinning reserve requirement\n    # sum(R_g) >= RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    min_reserves = RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n\n    A_ub_res = np.zeros((1, n_vars))\n    b_ub_res = np.array([min_reserves])\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        A_ub_res[0, i + n_gen] = -1.0  # -sum(R_g) >= -min_reserves\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    A_ub = np.vstack([A_ub_pmin, A_ub_pmax, A_ub_r_min, A_ub_r_max, A_ub_res])\n    b_ub = np.hstack([b_ub_pmin, b_ub_pmax, b_ub_r_min, b_ub_r_max, b_ub_res])\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    bounds = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((net['gen_pmin'][i], net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # P_g bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bounds.append((0, net['gen_pmax'][i]))  # R_g bounds\n\n    return c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds\n\ndef solve_dispatch(net):\n    \"\"\"Solve the economic dispatch problem\"\"\"\n    print(\"Building optimization problem...\")\n    c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds = build_dispatch_problem(net)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {len(c_obj)} variables, {len(b_ub)} inequality constraints, {len(b_eq)} equality constraints\")\n\n    # Debug: Check reserve requirement\n    total_load = np.sum(net['loads'])\n    min_reserves = RESERVE_FRACTION * total_load\n    print(f\"Total load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Minimum reserves required (5%): {min_reserves:.2f} MW\")\n\n    print(\"Solving optimization problem...\")\n    # Use linprog with sparse matrices for efficiency\n    result = linprog(\n        c=c_obj,\n        A_ub=A_ub if A_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        b_ub=b_ub if b_ub.size > 0 else None,\n        A_eq=A_eq if A_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        b_eq=b_eq if b_eq.size > 0 else None,\n        bounds=bounds,\n        method='highs',\n        options={'disp': False}\n    )\n\n    if result.success:\n        print(\"Optimization successful!\")\n        p_gen = result.x[:net['n_gen']]\n        r_gen = result.x[net['n_gen']:]\n\n        # Debug: Check reserves\n        total_reserves = np.sum(r_gen)\n        print(f\"Total reserves allocated: {total_reserves:.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"Number of generators with reserves > 0: {np.sum(r_gen > 0.1)}\")\n\n        # Check constraint violations\n        print(\"\\nChecking constraints:\")\n        print(f\"Power balance: sum(P_g) = {np.sum(p_gen):.2f}, should be {np.sum(net['loads']):.2f}\")\n        print(f\"Reserve requirement: sum(R_g) = {total_reserves:.2f}, should be >= {min_reserves:.2f}\")\n\n        # Check first few generators\n        print(\"\\nFirst 5 generators:\")\n        for i in range(min(5, len(p_gen))):\n            print(f\"  Gen {i+1}: P={p_gen[i]:.2f}, R={r_gen[i]:.2f}, Pmin={net['gen_pmin'][i]:.2f}, Pmax={net['gen_pmax'][i]:.2f}\")\n\n        return p_gen, r_gen, result\n    else:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n        return None, None, result\n\ndef calculate_line_flows(net, p_gen):\n    \"\"\"Calculate DC power flow and line loading\"\"\"\n    n_bus = net['n_bus']\n    n_branch = net['n_branch']\n    slack_bus = net['slack_bus']\n\n    # Build generator injection vector\n    P_gen_bus = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(net['gen_buses']):\n        P_gen_bus[int(bus) - 1] += p_gen[i]\n\n    # Build load vector\n    P_load_bus = net['loads']\n\n    # Net injection at each bus\n    P_net = P_gen_bus - P_load_bus\n\n    # Solve DC power flow: B * theta = P_net (excluding slack)\n    B = net['B']\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B, slack_bus, axis=0)\n    B_reduced = np.delete(B_reduced, slack_bus, axis=1)\n\n    P_net_reduced = np.delete(P_net, slack_bus)\n\n    # Solve for voltage angles\n    theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_net_reduced)\n    theta = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    theta[np.arange(n_bus) != slack_bus] = theta_reduced\n    theta[slack_bus] = 0\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    flows = []\n    from_bus = net['from_bus']\n    to_bus = net['to_bus']\n    rate_a = net['rate_a']\n    r = net.get('r', np.zeros(n_branch))\n    x = net.get('x', np.zeros(n_branch))\n\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        if from_bus[i] < n_bus and to_bus[i] < n_bus and x[i] != 0:\n            flow = (theta[int(from_bus[i])] - theta[int(to_bus[i])]) / x[i]\n            flow_mw = flow * net['base_mva']\n            loading_pct = abs(flow_mw / rate_a[i]) * 100 if rate_a[i] > 0 else 0\n\n            flows.append({\n                'from': int(from_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'to': int(to_bus[i]) + 1,\n                'flow_MW': flow_mw,\n                'rating_MVA': rate_a[i],\n                'loading_pct': loading_pct\n            })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting Grid Dispatch Economic Dispatch Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    #
